[Mac] Fix race in `InputTextPreventedSyncsCursorLocation`

`RenderWidgetHostViewMacTest.InputTextPreventedSyncsCursorLocation` was
disabled on macOS due to flakiness.

After navigating and calling `ExecJs()` to focus `<textarea id=ta>`, the
test immediately dispatched `[rwhv_cocoa keyEvent:key_a]` without:
1. Focusing the primary main frame and waiting for the asynchronous
   `TextInputStateChanged` update to set `rwhv_cocoa.textInputType` to
   `ui::TEXT_INPUT_TYPE_TEXT_AREA`, which is required for `[rwhv_cocoa
   inputContext]` to be non-nil during `[rwhv_cocoa
   interpretKeyEvents:]`.
2. Activating the widget's `InputRouter` via `MakeActive()`, matching
   the other `keyEvent:` tests in the same file, so
   `RenderInputRouter::FilterInputEvent()` does not drop the forwarded
   key events while post-navigation paint holding is active.

Focus the primary main frame, wait for `rwhv_cocoa.textInputType` to
reach `ui::TEXT_INPUT_TYPE_TEXT_AREA`, activate the `InputRouter` before
sending the key event, and re-enable the test. Also remove the unused
local class `InputMethodObserver` from the test.

Part of http://go/arthurmilchior-fix-all-test-skill-chrome for AI week.

Fixed: 421820726

6d3000feae9b387f865bae8d89d1caa3ca688592 [ diff ] 
[Wayland] Apply text-input-v3 deletions to the tracked text state

text-input-v3 specifies delete_surrounding_text lengths relative to the
current cursor, excluding the selection. ZwpTextInputV3Impl converted
them to an absolute range using the surrounding text it last sent, and
WaylandInputMethodContext then checked that range against the
SurroundingTextTracker's predicted state. These disagreed in two cases,
and the deletion was dropped with "The deletion range needs to cover
whole selection range.":

- The omnibox sets a reversed selection for inline autocompletion, and
  the absolute range was computed from the selection's start, which is
  its upper bound.
- Pending state is only sent after a done event whose serial matches
  the commit count, and wlroots does not send done for client commits.
  After a commit_string, the last sent surrounding text lags behind, so
  the range was computed from a stale cursor. Vietnamese Telex input in
  a web page produced "Vieẹt" instead of "Việt".

Forward the raw lengths to WaylandInputMethodContext and compute the
deletion around the predicted selection, or the preedit if present,
which already accounts for the input events applied since the
surrounding text was sent. This also removes the cached deletion range
bookkeeping from ZwpTextInputV3Impl.

The lagging surrounding text also hid the last commit from the input
method, so it could not compose with it, e.g. "ee" -> "ê". After
applying the input events of a done event, send the predicted
surrounding text before committing, as the protocol expects the state
sent in response to done to reflect the applied events. The update from
the text input client is then not sent again if it matches.

Fixed: 557316480

net: Enable OptimisticDnsForTcp and IPv6 fallback tuning by default

Enable the OptimisticDnsForTcp study features and parameters by default
on non-Cronet builds:
- kOptimisticDnsForTcp (with use_stale_connectors = true)
- kEnableIntermediateDnsResults (with SortTransactionsIndividually =
  true)
- kAdjustIPv6FallbackTime (with fallback_time = 100ms)
- kIPv6FallbackBasedOnRTT (with IPv6FallbackRTTMultiplier = 1.1)

Cronet remains excluded (matching kHappyEyeballsV2) because Cronet uses
TransportConnectJob and has not gathered field data for intermediate DNS
per-transaction sorting or the 100ms / 1.1x RTT IPv6 fallback timer.

Enabling kEnableIntermediateDnsResults with
SortTransactionsIndividually = true while kUseHostResolverCache is
disabled causes HostResolverDnsTask to sort AAAA responses individually
and skip the combined A+AAAA RFC 6724 sort on completion, so HostCache
entries always place sorted IPv6 endpoints ahead of IPv4 endpoints
(matching HostResolverCache and the 30% Stable experiment).

Remove the OptimisticDnsForTcp config from
fieldtrial_testing_config.json, allow fresh cache hits in
MockHostResolverBase::ResolveFromIPLiteralOrCache for
STALE_ALLOWED_WHILE_REFRESHING to match HostResolverManager, and add
unit tests covering HostCache with intermediate DNS results and the
kOptimisticDnsForTcp kill switch.

tree
08413dfabe8fab44f877c343f07f8706c9649065
parent
09f599cb373867d9dff20b37bfd1904eb5d65d37 [ diff ] 
Check proxy server ports and reject HTTP/0.9 from HTTP proxies

Validate proxy endpoints against IsPortAllowedForScheme() when starting
proxy connections and fail disallowed ports with
ERR_PROXY_CONNECTION_FAILED so standard proxy fallback applies.

Also reject statusless (HTTP/0.9) responses on non-tunneled HTTP proxy
requests with ERR_INVALID_HTTP_RESPONSE, including unencrypted legacy
Shoutcast 1.0 ("ICY 200") responses relayed verbatim over a configured
HTTP forward proxy.

Guarded by the "RestrictProxyPorts" and "RejectHttp09FromHttpProxy"
killswitches.

Bug: 574902296

[DC] No UI warning for UserInfoCredential requests

Previously, OpenID4VP SD-JWT DCQL requests for UserInfoCredential
triggered a caution UI warning hint when requesting user profile claims.
Allowlist UserInfoCredential in CanVctValueBypassInterstitial alongside
Phone Number Verification VCTs so UserInfoCredential requests return no
UI warning hint.

42fa16e74d533c33ea384a16d2c84aae4bae35f7
parent
58c3c3a4df3307ab4086611a7188b46574940fa8 [ diff ] 
[updater] Extract the libcurl network fetcher into its own files

Move the libcurl-based NetworkFetcher out of network_fetcher_linux.cc
into network_fetcher_libcurl.{h,cc}, leaving only the Linux
NetworkFetcherFactory behind. This lets other platforms build the
fetcher and lets it be unit tested without going through the factory.

This is a pure move with no behavior change. Follow-up CLs add an
explicit proxy, cancellation, timeouts and redirect handling to the
fetcher, and then build it on macOS as an alternative to the
NSURLSession fetcher for explicitly proxied downloads.

Bug: 581081910

[Autofill] Parse mock start date as UTC in `AutofillMetricsBaseTest`

In `FieldLogUkmMetricTest/LogFocusedComplexFormAtFormRemoveTest`,
`TestEmittedUKM` failed for `ablation_study` and
`ablation_study_in_dry_run_mode` when run on hosts in timezones east of
UTC (such as `Europe/Paris` or `Asia/Tokyo`), recording
`DayInAblationWindow` as `1` instead of `2`.

`AutofillMetricsBaseTest::SetUpHelper()` parsed `"01/01/20"` with
`base::Time::FromString()`, which assumes the host's local timezone. In
any timezone east of UTC, local midnight on 2020-01-01 corresponds to
2019-12-31 in UTC. When `LogFocusedComplexFormAtFormRemoveTest::SetUp()`
subsequently sets ICU's default timezone to `GMT`,
`DaysSinceLocalWindowsEpoch()` computes one fewer day since Windows
epoch, shifting `GetDayInAblationWindow()` from `2` to `1`.

Use `base::Time::FromUTCString()` so the mock clock always advances to
2020-01-01 00:00:00 UTC regardless of the host timezone. Verified
locally under `Europe/Paris`, `Asia/Tokyo`, `UTC`, and
`America/Los_Angeles`.

Test: components_unittests --gtest_filter=FieldLogUkmMetricTest/*
Change-Id: I47d3aff2eea7ae9c666822c4093c1d5490a0428c
Reviewed-on: https://chromium-review.googlesource.com/c/chromium/src/+/8814719
Auto-Submit: Arthur Milchior <arthurmilchior@chromium.org>
Commit-Queue: Jihad Hanna <jihadghanna@google.com>
Reviewed-by: Jihad Hanna <jihadghanna@google.com>
Cr-Commit-Position: refs/heads/main@{#1745075}

tree
a3db5251abf7f7ef5b37ca7174456c7dd6f05419
parent
151f95d2de718f78dad19a2153379a16f5762898 [ diff ] 
[SUIT] Use the server's FormattedString styles in Android suggestions

Android suggestions ignore the FormattedStrings in the server's
SuggestTemplateInfo, so the secondary text is never bolded and server
colors never show.

Apply the description classifications to the secondary text, as is
already done for the primary text. The parser builds them from the
server's FormattedString, so the secondary text gets the server's
bolding. When the primary or secondary text still matches the server's
FormattedString (dedupe can change it), also apply the fragment colors
to it. QUERY_TEXT, HINT_TEXT and ACTION_HIGH use the primary, secondary
and URL suggestion text colors, as the desktop searchbox does.
Secondary text bolding shows wherever the server bolds secondary text
today; colors show once the server sends them.

Fixed: b:579002567

a8aae5a45e2c69e405ed4caa5f6d2e73473a7ad7 [ diff ] 
[pip-ext] 18. Decouple TabsQueryFunction from BrowserWindowInterface

In TabsQueryFunction::BuildTabList() and MatchesWindow(), iterate
WindowControllerList::GetInstance() via WindowController* instead of
requiring a BrowserWindowInterface.

In MatchesTab(), handle null TabInterface and TabLifecycleUnitExternal
pointers for standalone WebContents.

Also default highlighted to true when TabInterface is null in
ExtensionTabUtil::CreateTabObject() and
TabsQueryFunction::MatchesTab().

MacInputAdapter::CocoaMouseCaptureDelegateImpl in the .mm file so that
NSEvent and NSWindow do not need to be double-declared in
tab_drag_session_input_adapter_mac.h.

🌺 Revert "WebContents: Hold WebContentsAndroid as JniRawPtr"

This reverts commit 72fb37791c6ff8de85162de3192919bc30f963c7.
Culprit: 72fb37791c6f - "WebContents: Hold WebContentsAndroid as JniRawPtr" (refs/heads/main@{#1744550}, https://crrev.com/c/8640229)

Reason for revert: consistently fails
android-14-tablet-landscape-arm64-rel.

Mechanism: Causes 89 CI failures across 13 builders and 16 try failures
across 9 distinct CLs (e.g.
NewTabPageTest#testFeedReliabilityLoggingHideWithBack). Holding
WebContentsAndroid as JniRawPtr enables strict LifetimeAssert checking
in @AfterClass, failing whenever tests non-deterministically leave
WebContents uncollected.

NewTabPageTest#testFeedReliabilityLoggingHideWithBack has failed in 89
recent builds of android-14-tablet-landscape-arm64-rel, every attempt in
every one of them. This is not flake: it reproduces on every run, so it
blocks the CQ for every unrelated CL that touches this suite. The
regression appears at refs/heads/main@{#1744550}.

Example failing run:
https://ci.chromium.org/b/8665199387579105169

To run/deflake this test on CQ before relanding, add the following
footer to your CL description:
  Include-Ci-Only-Tests: chromium.android:android-14-tablet-landscape-arm64-rel|chrome_public_test_apk

Reverting to green the tree first. Please re-land once the failure is
understood and covered; this revert is about restoring CQ throughput,
not about the merit of the change.

Original change's description:
> WebContents: Hold WebContentsAndroid as JniRawPtr
>
> WebContentsImpl stored the address of its C++ peer, WebContentsAndroid,
> as a raw long and passed it to about 90 native methods, and
> WebContents::FromJavaWebContents() read it back through
> getNativePointer() and reinterpret_cast. Any long that reached these
> entry points was trusted as a live WebContentsAndroid*.
>
> WebContentsAndroid owns the Java object's lifetime and always calls
> clearNativePtr() before it is deleted, so hold it as a nullable
> JniRawPtr<NativeWebContentsAndroid> and release and null it out there.
> Native methods take JniPtr<NativeWebContentsAndroid>, and
> getNativePointer() returns one, so FromJavaWebContents() still makes a
> single JNI call.
>
> Tests outside the package construct the object through the new
> WebContentsImpl.createForTesting(), which keeps the token
> package-private. Also destroy the standalone restored WebContents in
> BrowsingDataBridgeTest#testFrozenNavigationDeletion.
>
> Bug: 40794873
> Change-Id: I0d36faaa5e71d34b9b86b2ffd6eec432555b10ae

[Agentic Suggestions] Support SUIT TypeIconBadge on Desktop

In arm 2 of the agentic suggestions experiment, desktop shows a small
blue dot on the top-right corner of the history icon on the left of the
matches in Realbox and the omnibox popup. The server sends it as
SuggestTemplateInfo.type_icon_badge.

Copy the TypeIconBadge proto change from google3, pass the badge through
the SuggestTemplateInfo mojom, and draw the dot on the searchbox history
icon.

Screenshot: https://screenshot.corp.google.com/1b7o5tm6e3msg

48210d6c30a52dfe0402dadb1f720b69d51f7e28
parent
b72b65bf937c7b940b2d91567c291ae2d435e987 [ diff ] 
[iOS Blink] Fix build break in CRCWebViewProxyImpl

https://crrev.com/c/8689594 added setZoomSize: to the CRWWebViewProxy
protocol, but CRCWebViewProxyImpl was not updated, so the iOS Blink
build fails with -Wprotocol. The WebKit implementation forwards the call
to web::provider::SetZoomForWebView(), which takes a WKWebView that this
proxy doesn't have. This CL returns NO to report that the zoom was not
set, as the protocol allows for platforms where it isn't supported.

4bd8c095f2040a7401f7f55bd98a92c8166eb850
parent
407a6be58c4da5e0d39d2549f4a338073dff2a96 [ diff ] 
[window.rust] Add scoped Rust crash keys

Adds `CrashKeyString::set_scoped()`, which returns a
`ScopedCrashKeyString` guard that clears the crash key when dropped
(the Rust equivalent of crash_reporter::ScopedCrashKeyString), and a
`scoped_crash_key!` macro that mirrors SCOPED_CRASH_KEY_STRING*:

  scoped_crash_key!("Category", "name", 256, value);
  base::debug::DumpWithoutCrashing();

The macro declares a static crash key named "Category-name" and binds
the guard to a hygienic local, so the key stays set until the end of the
enclosing block.

8aa25b93f1ca862d3e99057e56cfc33ceb525115 [ diff ] 
[Android] Inline ViewUtils to ViewFinder in settings tests

Inlines onViewWaiting() and waitForVisibleView() to
ViewFinder.waitForView() in settings, site settings, browsing data,
page info, permissions, privacy guide and safety hub tests.

ViewFinder already waits for the View to be VISIBLE, >= 51% displayed
and enabled, so this also removes the now-redundant isDisplayed()
matchers and check(matches(isDisplayed())) assertions, replaces
isCompletelyDisplayed() with displayingAtLeastOption(100), and folds
wait-then-click sequences into waitForView().click().

Bug: 481024569

Move SetWantErrorMessageStackTrace mojo method to LocalFrame

Move SetWantErrorMessageStackTrace from content.mojom.Frame to
blink.mojom.LocalFrame and handle it directly within Blink.

This allows tracking whether stack traces are requested for error
console messages in LocalFrame and FrameConsole directly. As a result,
WebLocalFrameClient::ShouldReportDetailedMessageForSourceAndSeverity is
simplified to ShouldReportDetailedMessageForSource, removing the log
level check from the embedder interface.

Remove unimplemented CancelInitialHistoryLoad IPC

RenderFrameHostImpl::CancelInitialHistoryLoad was never implemented and
only contained a NOTIMPLEMENTED() call. This change removes the
CancelInitialHistoryLoad method from frame.mojom, cleans up the call
site in RenderFrameImpl, and removes the method implementations and test
stubs.

PaymentHandlerHost: Adopt JniUniquePtr
📋 Metadata
commit
1df9c471728f2902e8b94119a8c1cb06abe7302c
[ log ]
author
Keigo Oka <oka@google.com>
Sat Nov 14 23:33:03 2026
committer
Chromium LUCI CQ <chromium-scoped@luci-project-accounts.iam.gserviceaccount.com>
Sat Nov 14 23:33:03 2026
tree
96f93c50dfefe58ca73ac9c3080686ebcfe4d386
parent
8999a25190b5252cfb419516a28b9e38d1c5f4ce [ diff ] 
PaymentHandlerHost: Adopt JniUniquePtr

PaymentHandlerHost stores its native
::payments::android::PaymentHandlerHost pointer in raw long field
mNativePointer. Passing raw integer handles across the JNI boundary
bypasses type checking and smart pointer lifecycle enforcement, risking
use-after-free bugs if the native object is accessed after destruction.

Migrate mNativePointer to JniUniquePtr with a package-private
JniTypeToken mapped to ::payments::android::PaymentHandlerHost via
@JniType. Return jni_zero::MakeUnique from the C++ factory, destroy the
owned instance via JniUniquePtr.destroy(), and pass JniPtr to
@NativeMethods calls. The field stays set after destroy(), so a later
native call throws IllegalStateException instead of passing a null
pointer to native.

Bug: 40794873

[TTC] Let get_pointer fall back to the user's text selection

On a tablet there is no pointer, so get_pointer answers "what is this?"
with no_active_pointer. Selecting text is how a user without a pointer
singles something out.

Add a param to kTtcGetPointer, use_selection, off by default. With it
on, when the user is not pointing but has selected text in the page's
main document, the tool's image shows the page around the selection,
without a crosshair, and its answer has the selected text and where it
is in place of the points.

The selection is read from the page content that the tool captures
anyway, so it works the same on every platform and matches the
screenshot. Its bounds are those of the content nodes it spans, which is
enough to pick what to show. A selection that is scrolled out of view,
or inside a text field, whose text is not a node of the page content,
does not count. Neither does one without text, such as a selection of
only an image, for now (crbug.com/585958593).

Whether there is a selection is only known after the capture, so with
the param on the tool captures the page even when the user is not
pointing.

Design: go/ttc-get-pointer (CL D3 of the plan)

    --gtest_filter=ToolControllerGetPointerSelectionBrowserTest.*

Bug: 571268412
Test: unit_tests --gtest_filter=GetPointerResponse*
Test: browser_tests / android_browsertests

b1a35ce39a95844b9fd19040b434b0a5b7a3a3ea
parent
d5085d774d2462125f2ee423e495601dc0b72cf5 [ diff ] 
[Android] Forget earlier tests' windows at the start of a browser test

TabModelJniBridgeTest.AddToGroupWithOutOfOrderHandles and
PersistedTabDataAndroidBrowserTest.TestDeferredStartup fail in the first
attempt of most builds on the Android emulator bots, because the browser
starts with two tabs instead of one.

The extra tab comes from the test before them. The browser keeps a
record of its windows, and the state of their tabs, in the app's data
directory, outside the user data dir that ChromeBrowserTestsActivity
deletes before each test. A window that a test closes stays on that
record as a recently closed one. The activity of the next test is then
given that window instead of a new one ("Existing Instance - persisted
and unmapped Id allocated" in logcat) and starts with a tab too many.

This reproduces on an emulator after
DwaBrowserTest.IncognitoPlusRegularBrowserCheck, which closes its
browsers. A bot's logcat shows the same after
ActorWindowManagementToolBrowserTest.CloseWindow.

Forget the recorded windows and delete the tab state before each test,
unless the test keeps its user data dir, as the test after a PRE_ test
does. This is done in onPreCreate(), because the activity is given its
window before performPreInflationStartup(), where the user data dir is
deleted. The record is reset with
MultiInstancePersistentStore.resetForTesting(), which empties it in
memory and deletes its file.

Deleting the tab state is not needed for the two tests above. Without
it, the tabs of a window that a test closed would stay on disk for the
next window that is given the same id.

    DwaBrowserTest.IncognitoPlusRegularBrowserCheck and
    TabModelJniBridgeTest.AddToGroupWithOutOfOrderHandles in one run;
    the second fails without this change

Bug: 585643944, 585642498
Test: android_browsertests on an Android 12L emulator, with

[unsafe-buffer] Spanify AV1RateControl::ConvertControlConfig

ConvertControlConfig() wrote into the C arrays of
aom::AV1RateControlRtcConfig with variable indices, which needed six
UNSAFE_TODO() markers. Index them through base::span instead, so every
write is bounds-checked. The loops and the copied values are unchanged.

Fixed: 490873562

parent
c8b584b00be0aa9d69353cd984bdef474b771877 [ diff ] 
url: Fix non-ASCII code points being rejected as forbidden hosts

IsForbiddenHostCodePoint() took a uint8_t, so a char16_t code unit such
as U+9500 was narrowed to 0x00 (NUL) and misreported as a forbidden host
code point. Non-special URLs whose opaque host contains such non-ASCII
characters (e.g. Chinese text) then failed to parse with "Invalid URL".

Change the parameter to char16_t and return early for non-ASCII code
points, which are never forbidden per the URL Standard.

Bug: 573812509

89001c49f8b11584aa6c9ec2ad16a67b671f6c42 [ diff ] 
[remoting] Scale VP9 encoder threads using max frame dimension

Moves `g_threads` initialization into the per-codec helpers and passes
`std::max(config->g_w, config->g_h)` to `GetEncoderThreadCount()` in
`SetVp9CodecParameters()`, matching AV1. Although `libvpx` clamps
column tiles to the frame width, `VP9E_SET_ROW_MT` parallelizes 64x64
superblock rows across worker threads within each tile column,
allowing portrait displays to utilize additional threads.

On a 48-core / 96-thread Intel Xeon, portrait VP9 encode latency drops
by 30%-58% across 1080x1920 (2 -> 8 threads) and 2160x3840 (8 -> 16
threads).

Change-Id: I9c4d1b8e2f0a5c7d3e6b1f8a2c4d9e7b0f3a6c1d
Reviewed-on: https://chromium-review.googlesource.com/c/chromium/src/+/8694956

Commit-Queue: Joe Downing <joedow@chromium.org>
